check the READ sites, a later function reading `lfs` out of the block proved it a mixed struct). **Bodies right but ordered wrong** → flip **which side of the condition is the then-branch** (three separate instances this session: `so`'s `modeEventFirstWait`, `ob1`'s `control_anmAtr`, `aj1`'s `next_msgStatus`). **Jump-table cases in the wrong order** → source case order is the **body ADDRESS order**, not numeric — and read the bodies, because a body ending WITHOUT a branch is a deliberate `case` fall-through (`aj1`'s `talk_1`, 0x9DA → 0x9C8). **A global re-loaded at each use** → the donor **re-calls the accessor**; do not hoist it into a local (cost me 11 rows in `chk_areaIN` by caching `dComIfGp_getPlayer(0)`). — **⚠ AND THE ONE THAT NEVER PAYS: REGISTER COLOURING.** Roughly **fifteen measured attempts across `so`, `ob1`, `p2` and `aj1`, zero successes.** Declaration order, local aliasing, hoisting, the byte-vs-int type changes that fix everything else, the matching-sibling "declare locals up front" arrangement (measured 42 → 45 rows, WORSE) — all null. **Treat a colouring-only diff as a park candidate, not a puzzle.** That is the evidence behind the park proposal still sitting open on row 440. — **WHY THIS ROW EXISTS:** the same ten symptoms recur across every NPC TU, and each one I had to rediscover cost 2-16 rows and several builds. **Any lane starting a fresh actor should read the bank BEFORE writing, not after stalling.** Nothing is asked of anyone here — this is method. **The one thing still owed TO me is History's `dNpc_HIO_c` vtable scoping (rows 437/439/440), which remains the only item on `so` with a known cause AND a known fix.**

A : A-1` computes 0/+1 and adds A-1; `x == 0 ? A-1 : A` computes 0/-1 and adds A — if your added constant is off by one, flip the ternary); an **explicit empty `case`** shows only in the dispatch tree; a **stale float register at a call site is a real argument** (`shadowDraw` never reloads `f3` because `current.pos.y` IS the third float arg); **frame size too small means a missing or wrongly-`static` local**; and the `extsb`-vs-`cmplwi` **`s8` signal has now paid five times on one TU** — one field fix closed two functions at once. — **THE ONE THAT STILL NEVER PAYS: register colouring.** Now ~17 measured attempts across four TUs, zero successes; the newest failures are holding a member in a named local (`setMtx` 8 → 18 rows, frame grew) and the matching-sibling declare-locals-up-front arrangement (`lookBack` 42 → 45). **Unchanged recommendation: colouring-only diffs are park candidates, not puzzles** — which is what row 440 is still waiting on.
